           │ class Module:                                                      │
           │     """An abstract class to represent a UCLID5 module."""          │
           │                                                                    │
           │     def types(self):                                               │
           │         """(Optional) Defines the type declarations.               │
           │         For example, the following implementation defines a 8-bit  │
           │ type called T:                                                     │
           │         ```                                                        │
           │         def types(self):                                           │
           │             self.T = BitVector(8)                                  │
           │         ```                                                        │
           │         """                                                        │
           │         pass                                                       │
           │                                                                    │
           │     def locals(self):                                              │
           │         """(Optional) Defines the local variables and their types. │
           │         For example, the following implementation defines an 8-bit │
           │ variable x                                                         │
           │         and an integer variable y:                                 │
           │         ```                                                        │
           │         def locals(self):                                          │
           │             self.x = BitVector(8)                                  │
           │             self.y = Integer()                                     │
           │         ```                                                        │
           │         """                                                        │
           │         pass                                                       │
           │                                                                    │
           │     def inputs(self):                                              │
           │         """(Optional) Defines the input variables and their types. │
           │         For example, the following implementation defines an input │
           │ variable x,                                                        │
           │         which is an array of 8-bit bitvectors indexed by 2-bit     │
           │ bitvectors:                                                        │
           │         ```                                                        │
           │         def inputs(self):                                          │
           │             self.x = Array(BitVector(2), BitVector(8))             │
           │         ```                                                        │
           │         """                                                        │
           │         pass                                                       │
           │                                                                    │
           │     def outputs(self):                                             │
           │         """(Optional) Defines the output variables and their       │
           │ types.                                                             │
           │         For example, the following implementation defines an       │
           │ output variable y,                                                 │
           │         which is a real number:                                    │
           │         ```                                                        │
           │         def outputs(self):                                         │
           │             self.y = Real()                                        │
           │         ```                                                        │
           │         """                                                        │
           │         pass                                                       │
           │                                                                    │
           │     def shared_vars(self):                                         │
           │         """(Optional) Defines the shared variables and their       │
           │ types.                                                             │
           │         For example, the following implementation defines a shared │
           │ variable z,                                                        │
           │         which is an array of booleans indexed by integers:         │
           │         ```                                                        │
           │         def shared_vars(self):                                     │
           │             self.z = Array(Integer(), Boolean())                   │
           │         ```                                                        │
           │         """                                                        │
           │         pass                                                       │
           │                                                                    │
           │     def instances(self):                                           │
           │         """(Optional) Defines the instances of other modules and   │
           │ relates their                                                      │
           │         input, output, and shared variables to local variables.    │
           │ Every instance                                                     │
           │         variable must be related to a local variable. For example, │
           │ let M be                                                           │
           │         another module with inputs x and y, and output z. The      │
           │ following                                                          │
           │         implementation defines an instance of M called m, and      │
           │ connects M's                                                       │
           │         input variable x to the local variable self.a, M's input   │
           │ variable y to                                                      │
           │         the local variable self.b, and M's output variable z to    │
           │ the local                                                          │
           │         variable self.c:                                           │
           │         ```                                                        │
           │         def instances(self):                                       │
           │             self.m = M(x=self.a, y=self.b, z=self.c)               │
           │         ```                                                        │
           │         """                                                        │
           │         pass                                                       │
           │                                                                    │
           │     def init(self):                                                │
           │         """(Optional) Defines how variables are initialized.       │
           │         For example, the following implementation initializes x to │
           │ 0 if y is                                                          │
           │         greater than or equal to 20:                               │
           │         ```                                                        │
           │         def init(self):                                            │
           │             if self.y >= 20:                                       │
           │                 self.x = 0                                         │
           │         ```                                                        │
           │         """                                                        │
           │         pass                                                       │
           │                                                                    │
           │     def next(self):                                                │
           │         """(Optional) Defines the transition relation.             │
           │         For example, the following implementation increments x or  │
           │ decrements y                                                       │
           │         depending on z:                                            │
           │         ```                                                        │
           │         def next(self):                                            │
           │             if self.z:                                             │
           │                 self.x = self.x + 1                                │
           │             else:                                                  │
           │                 self.y = self.y - 1                                │
           │         ```                                                        │
           │         """                                                        │
           │         pass                                                       │
           │                                                                    │
           │     def specification(self):                                       │
           │         """(Required) Defines the specification in terms of        │
           │ invariant properties.                                              │
           │                                                                    │
           │         Returns:                                                   │
           │             bool: True if the specification is satisfied, False    │
           │ otherwise.                                                         │
           │                                                                    │
           │         For example, the following implementation defines two      │
           │ invariants:                                                        │
           │         ```                                                        │
           │         def specification(self):                                   │
           │             return self.x < 10 and self.y > 0                      │
           │                                                                    │
           │         DO NOT JUST RETURN THE VARIABLE TYPES!                     │
           │         """                                                        │
           │         raise NotImplementedError                                  │
           │ ```                                                                │

           │ INC(x) and STORE(x). Consider now the parallel program: x := 0; P1 │
           │ || P2 || P3 Build a model of the parallel program. Write a         │
           │ property that checks if P has an execution that halts with the     │
           │ terminal value x=2. Use the variable name x.                       │
           │                                                                    │
           │ Reply with your Python code inside one unique code block.          │
           │                                                                    │

           │ class Module:                                                      │
           │     """An abstract class to represent a UCLID5 module."""          │
           │                                                                    │
           │     def types(self):                                               │
           │         """(Optional) Defines the type declarations.               │
           │         For example, the following implementation defines a 8-bit  │
           │ type called T:                                                     │
           │         ```                                                        │
           │         def types(self):                                           │
           │             self.T = BitVector(8)                                  │
           │         ```                                                        │
           │         """                                                        │
           │         pass                                                       │
           │                                                                    │
           │     def locals(self):                                              │
           │         """(Optional) Defines the local variables and their types. │
           │         For example, the following implementation defines an 8-bit │

           │         and an integer variable y:                                 │
           │         ```                                                        │

           │         ```                                                        │
           │         """                                                        │
           │         pass                                                       │
           │                                                                    │
           │     def inputs(self):                                              │
           │         """(Optional) Defines the input variables and their types. │
           │         For example, the following implementation defines an input │
           │ variable x,                                                        │
           │         which is an array of 8-bit bitvectors indexed by 2-bit     │
           │ bitvectors:                                                        │

           │             self.x = Array(BitVector(2), BitVector(8))             │
           │         ```                                                        │
           │         """                                                        │
           │         pass                                                       │
           │                                                                    │
           │     def outputs(self):                                             │
           │         """(Optional) Defines the output variables and their       │
           │ types.                                                             │
           │         For example, the following implementation defines an       │

           │         ```                                                        │
           │         """                                                        │
           │         pass                                                       │
           │                                                                    │
           │     def instances(self):                                           │

           │ relates their                                                      │
           │         input, output, and shared variables to local variables.    │
           │ Every instance                                                     │
           │         variable must be related to a local variable. For example, │

           │         another module with inputs x and y, and output z. The      │
           │ following                                                          │
           │         implementation defines an instance of M called m, and      │
           │ connects M's                                                       │
           │         input variable x to the local variable self.a, M's input   │
           │ variable y to                                                      │
           │         the local variable self.b, and M's output variable z to    │
           │ the local                                                          │
           │         variable self.c:                                           │
           │         ```                                                        │
           │         def instances(self):                                       │
           │             self.m = M(x=self.a, y=self.b, z=self.c)               │
           │         ```                                                        │
           │         """                                                        │
           │         pass                                                       │
           │                                                                    │
           │     def init(self):                                                │

           │         greater than or equal to 20:                               │
           │         ```                                                        │

           │                                                                    │
           │     def types(self):                                               │
           │         """(Optional) Defines the type declarations.               │
           │         For example, the following implementation defines a 8-bit  │
           │ type called T:                                                     │
           │         ```                                                        │

           │         ```                                                        │
           │         """                                                        │
           │         pass                                                       │
           │                                                                    │
           │     def locals(self):                                              │
           │         """(Optional) Defines the local variables and their types. │
           │         For example, the following implementation defines an 8-bit │
           │ variable x                                                         │
           │         and an integer variable y:                                 │
           │         ```                                                        │

           │             self.y = Integer()                                     │
           │         ```                                                        │
           │         """                                                        │
           │         pass                                                       │
           │                                                                    │

           │         """(Optional) Defines the input variables and their types. │
           │         For example, the following implementation defines an input │
           │ variable x,                                                        │
           │         which is an array of 8-bit bitvectors indexed by 2-bit     │
           │ bitvectors:                                                        │

           │         ```                                                        │
           │         """                                                        │
           │         pass                                                       │
           │                                                                    │

           │         """(Optional) Defines the output variables and their       │
           │ types.                                                             │
           │         For example, the following implementation defines an       │
           │ output variable y,                                                 │
           │         which is a real number:                                    │
           │         ```                                                        │
           │         def outputs(self):                                         │
           │             self.y = Real()                                        │
           │         ```                                                        │
           │         """                                                        │
           │         pass                                                       │

           │         ```                                                        │
           │         """                                                        │
           │         pass                                                       │
           │                                                                    │
           │     def instances(self):                                           │
           │         """(Optional) Defines the instances of other modules and   │

           │ Every instance                                                     │
           │         variable must be related to a local variable. For example, │

           │         another module with inputs x and y, and output z. The      │
           │ following                                                          │
           │         implementation defines an instance of M called m, and      │
           │ connects M's                                                       │
           │         input variable x to the local variable self.a, M's input   │

           │         the local variable self.b, and M's output variable z to    │
           │ the local                                                          │
           │         variable self.c:                                           │
           │         ```                                                        │
           │         def instances(self):                                       │
           │             self.m = M(x=self.a, y=self.b, z=self.c)               │
           │         ```                                                        │
           │         """                                                        │
           │         pass                                                       │
           │                                                                    │
           │     def init(self):                                                │
           │         """(Optional) Defines how variables are initialized.       │
           │         For example, the following implementation initializes x to │
           │ 0 if y is                                                          │
           │         greater than or equal to 20:                               │
           │         ```                                                        │

           │ > We are given three (primitive) processes P1,P2, and P3 with      │

           │ class Module:                                                      │
           │     """An abstract class to represent a UCLID5 module."""          │
           │                                                                    │
           │     def types(self):                                               │
           │         """(Optional) Defines the type declarations.               │
           │         For example, the following implementation defines a 8-bit  │
           │ type called T:                                                     │
           │         ```                                                        │
           │         def types(self):                                           │
           │             self.T = BitVector(8)                                  │
           │         ```                                                        │
           │         """                                                        │
           │         pass                                                       │
           │                                                                    │
           │     def locals(self):                                              │
           │         """(Optional) Defines the local variables and their types. │
           │         For example, the following implementation defines an 8-bit │
           │ variable x                                                         │
           │         and an integer variable y:                                 │
           │         ```                                                        │
           │         def locals(self):                                          │
           │             self.x = BitVector(8)                                  │
           │             self.y = Integer()                                     │
           │         ```                                                        │
           │         """                                                        │
           │         pass                                                       │
           │                                                                    │
           │     def inputs(self):                                              │
           │         """(Optional) Defines the input variables and their types. │
           │         For example, the following implementation defines an input │
           │ variable x,                                                        │
           │         which is an array of 8-bit bitvectors indexed by 2-bit     │
           │ bitvectors:                                                        │
           │         ```                                                        │
           │         def inputs(self):                                          │
           │             self.x = Array(BitVector(2), BitVector(8))             │
           │         ```                                                        │
           │         """                                                        │
           │         pass                                                       │
           │                                                                    │
           │     def outputs(self):                                             │
           │         """(Optional) Defines the output variables and their       │
           │ types.                                                             │
           │         For example, the following implementation defines an       │
           │ output variable y,                                                 │
           │         which is a real number:                                    │
           │         ```                                                        │
           │         def outputs(self):                                         │
           │             self.y = Real()                                        │
           │         ```                                                        │
           │         """                                                        │
           │         pass                                                       │
           │                                                                    │
           │     def shared_vars(self):                                         │
           │         """(Optional) Defines the shared variables and their       │
           │ types.                                                             │
           │         For example, the following implementation defines a shared │
           │ variable z,                                                        │
           │         which is an array of booleans indexed by integers:         │
           │         ```                                                        │
           │         def shared_vars(self):                                     │
           │             self.z = Array(Integer(), Boolean())                   │
           │         ```                                                        │
           │         """                                                        │
           │         pass                                                       │
           │                                                                    │
           │     def instances(self):                                           │
           │         """(Optional) Defines the instances of other modules and   │
           │ relates their                                                      │
           │         input, output, and shared variables to local variables.    │
           │ Every instance                                                     │
           │         variable must be related to a local variable. For example, │
           │ let M be                                                           │
           │         another module with inputs x and y, and output z. The      │
           │ following                                                          │
           │         implementation defines an instance of M called m, and      │
           │ connects M's                                                       │
           │         input variable x to the local variable self.a, M's input   │
           │ variable y to                                                      │
           │         the local variable self.b, and M's output variable z to    │
           │ the local                                                          │
           │         variable self.c:                                           │
           │         ```                                                        │
           │         def instances(self):                                       │
           │             self.m = M(x=self.a, y=self.b, z=self.c)               │
           │         ```                                                        │
           │         """                                                        │
           │         pass                                                       │
           │                                                                    │
           │     def init(self):                                                │
           │         """(Optional) Defines how variables are initialized.       │
           │         For example, the following implementation initializes x to │
           │ 0 if y is                                                          │
           │         greater than or equal to 20:                               │
           │         ```                                                        │
           │         def init(self):                                            │
           │             if self.y >= 20:                                       │
           │                 self.x = 0                                         │
           │         ```                                                        │
           │         """                                                        │
           │         pass                                                       │
           │                                                                    │
           │     def next(self):                                                │
           │         """(Optional) Defines the transition relation.             │
           │         For example, the following implementation increments x or  │
           │ decrements y                                                       │
           │         depending on z:                                            │
           │         ```                                                        │
           │         def next(self):                                            │
           │             if self.z:                                             │
           │                 self.x = self.x + 1                                │
           │             else:                                                  │
           │                 self.y = self.y - 1                                │
           │         ```                                                        │
           │         """                                                        │
           │         pass                                                       │
           │                                                                    │
           │     def specification(self):                                       │
           │         """(Required) Defines the specification in terms of        │
           │ invariant properties.                                              │
           │                                                                    │
           │         Returns:                                                   │
           │             bool: True if the specification is satisfied, False    │
           │ otherwise.                                                         │
           │                                                                    │
           │         For example, the following implementation defines two      │
           │ invariants:                                                        │
           │         ```                                                        │
           │         def specification(self):                                   │
           │             return self.x < 10 and self.y > 0                      │
           │                                                                    │

           │ variable x,                                                        │
           │         which is an array of 8-bit bitvectors indexed by 2-bit     │
           │ bitvectors:                                                        │
           │         ```                                                        │
           │         def inputs(self):                                          │
           │             self.x = Array(BitVector(2), BitVector(8))             │
           │         ```                                                        │
           │         """                                                        │
           │         pass                                                       │
           │                                                                    │
           │     def outputs(self):                                             │
           │         """(Optional) Defines the output variables and their       │
           │ types.                                                             │
           │         For example, the following implementation defines an       │
           │ output variable y,                                                 │
           │         which is a real number:                                    │
           │         ```                                                        │
           │         def outputs(self):                                         │
           │             self.y = Real()                                        │
           │         ```                                                        │
           │         """                                                        │
           │         pass                                                       │
           │                                                                    │
           │     def shared_vars(self):                                         │
           │         """(Optional) Defines the shared variables and their       │
           │ types.                                                             │
           │         For example, the following implementation defines a shared │
           │ variable z,                                                        │
           │         which is an array of booleans indexed by integers:         │
           │         ```                                                        │
           │         def shared_vars(self):                                     │
           │             self.z = Array(Integer(), Boolean())                   │
           │         ```                                                        │
           │         """                                                        │
           │         pass                                                       │
           │                                                                    │
           │     def instances(self):                                           │
           │         """(Optional) Defines the instances of other modules and   │
           │ relates their                                                      │
           │         input, output, and shared variables to local variables.    │
           │ Every instance                                                     │
           │         variable must be related to a local variable. For example, │
           │ let M be                                                           │
           │         another module with inputs x and y, and output z. The      │
           │ following                                                          │
           │         implementation defines an instance of M called m, and      │
           │ connects M's                                                       │
           │         input variable x to the local variable self.a, M's input   │
           │ variable y to                                                      │
           │         the local variable self.b, and M's output variable z to    │
           │ the local                                                          │
           │         variable self.c:                                           │
           │         ```                                                        │
           │         def instances(self):                                       │
           │             self.m = M(x=self.a, y=self.b, z=self.c)               │
           │         ```                                                        │
           │         """                                                        │
           │         pass                                                       │
           │                                                                    │
           │     def init(self):                                                │
           │         """(Optional) Defines how variables are initialized.       │
           │         For example, the following implementation initializes x to │
           │ 0 if y is                                                          │
           │         greater than or equal to 20:                               │
           │         ```                                                        │
           │         def init(self):                                            │
           │             if self.y >= 20:                                       │
           │                 self.x = 0                                         │
           │         ```                                                        │
           │         """                                                        │
           │         pass                                                       │
           │                                                                    │
           │     def next(self):                                                │
           │         """(Optional) Defines the transition relation.             │
           │         For example, the following implementation increments x or  │
           │ decrements y                                                       │
           │         depending on z:                                            │
           │         ```                                                        │
           │         def next(self):                                            │
           │             if self.z:                                             │
           │                 self.x = self.x + 1                                │
           │             else:                                                  │
           │                 self.y = self.y - 1                                │
           │         ```                                                        │
           │         """                                                        │
           │         pass                                                       │
           │                                                                    │
           │     def specification(self):                                       │
           │         """(Required) Defines the specification in terms of        │

           │                                                                    │
           │         Returns:                                                   │

           │     """An abstract class to represent a UCLID5 module."""          │
           │                                                                    │
           │     def types(self):                                               │
           │         """(Optional) Defines the type declarations.               │
           │         For example, the following implementation defines a 8-bit  │
           │ type called T:                                                     │
           │         ```                                                        │
           │         def types(self):                                           │

           │         ```                                                        │
           │         """                                                        │
           │         pass                                                       │
           │                                                                    │
           │     def locals(self):                                              │
           │         """(Optional) Defines the local variables and their types. │
           │         For example, the following implementation defines an 8-bit │
           │ variable x                                                         │
           │         and an integer variable y:                                 │
           │         ```                                                        │
           │         def locals(self):                                          │
           │             self.x = BitVector(8)                                  │
           │             self.y = Integer()                                     │
           │         ```                                                        │
           │         """                                                        │
           │         pass                                                       │
           │                                                                    │
           │     def inputs(self):                                              │
           │         """(Optional) Defines the input variables and their types. │
           │         For example, the following implementation defines an input │
           │ variable x,                                                        │
           │         which is an array of 8-bit bitvectors indexed by 2-bit     │
           │ bitvectors:                                                        │
           │         ```                                                        │

           │             self.x = Array(BitVector(2), BitVector(8))             │
           │         ```                                                        │
           │         """                                                        │
           │         pass                                                       │
           │                                                                    │
           │     def outputs(self):                                             │
           │         """(Optional) Defines the output variables and their       │
           │ types.                                                             │
           │         For example, the following implementation defines an       │
           │ output variable y,                                                 │
           │         which is a real number:                                    │
           │         ```                                                        │
           │         def outputs(self):                                         │
           │             self.y = Real()                                        │
           │         ```                                                        │
           │         """                                                        │
           │         pass                                                       │
           │                                                                    │
           │     def shared_vars(self):                                         │
           │         """(Optional) Defines the shared variables and their       │
           │ types.                                                             │
           │         For example, the following implementation defines a shared │
           │ variable z,                                                        │
           │         which is an array of booleans indexed by integers:         │
           │         ```                                                        │
           │         def shared_vars(self):                                     │
           │             self.z = Array(Integer(), Boolean())                   │
           │         ```                                                        │
           │         """                                                        │
           │         pass                                                       │
           │                                                                    │
           │     def instances(self):                                           │
           │         """(Optional) Defines the instances of other modules and   │
           │ relates their                                                      │
           │         input, output, and shared variables to local variables.    │
           │ Every instance                                                     │
           │         variable must be related to a local variable. For example, │
           │ let M be                                                           │
           │         another module with inputs x and y, and output z. The      │
           │ following                                                          │
           │         implementation defines an instance of M called m, and      │
           │ connects M's                                                       │
           │         input variable x to the local variable self.a, M's input   │
           │ variable y to                                                      │
           │         the local variable self.b, and M's output variable z to    │
           │ the local                                                          │
           │         variable self.c:                                           │
           │         ```                                                        │
           │         def instances(self):                                       │
           │             self.m = M(x=self.a, y=self.b, z=self.c)               │
           │         ```                                                        │
           │         """                                                        │
           │         pass                                                       │
           │                                                                    │
           │     def init(self):                                                │
           │         """(Optional) Defines how variables are initialized.       │
           │         For example, the following implementation initializes x to │
           │ 0 if y is                                                          │
           │         greater than or equal to 20:                               │
           │         ```                                                        │

           │         depending on z:                                            │

           │     def types(self):                                               │
           │         """(Optional) Defines the type declarations.               │
           │         For example, the following implementation defines a 8-bit  │

           │         pass                                                       │
           │                                                                    │

           │         """                                                        │

           │         which is an array of 8-bit bitvectors indexed by 2-bit     │

           │         ```                                                        │
           │         """                                                        │
           │         pass                                                       │
           │                                                                    │
           │     def outputs(self):                                             │
           │         """(Optional) Defines the output variables and their       │
           │ types.                                                             │
           │         For example, the following implementation defines an       │
           │ output variable y,                                                 │
           │         which is a real number:                                    │
           │         ```                                                        │
           │         def outputs(self):                                         │

           │         """                                                        │
           │         pass                                                       │

           │         """                                                        │
           │         pass                                                       │

           │ Every instance                                                     │

           │ following                                                          │
           │         implementation defines an instance of M called m, and      │
           │ connects M's                                                       │
           │         input variable x to the local variable self.a, M's input   │
           │ variable y to                                                      │
           │         the local variable self.b, and M's output variable z to    │
           │ the local                                                          │
           │         variable self.c:                                           │

           │         def instances(self):                                       │
           │             self.m = M(x=self.a, y=self.b, z=self.c)               │
           │         ```                                                        │
           │         """                                                        │
           │         pass                                                       │
           │                                                                    │
           │     def init(self):                                                │

           │         greater than or equal to 20:                               │
           │         ```                                                        │

           │ block: Analysis of next function and logic problems                │
           │ fix: The major issue with the model is that the `next` function is │
           │ not properly representing the parallel nature of the processes P1, │
           │ P2, and P3. Each process should independently increment the shared │
           │ variable `x`, simulating the `LOAD`, `INC`, and `STORE` operations │
           │ ten times each. However, the current `next` function simply        │
           │ increments `x` when it is less than 10, which doesn't account for  │
           │ the distinct actions each process should perform.                  │
           │                                                                    │
           │ The `next` function should be extended to include the `LOAD`,      │
           │ `INC`, and `STORE` logic in a way that allows all three processes  │
           │ to potentially execute these operations in parallel on the shared  │
           │ `x` variable. This requires a mechanism to ensure that each        │
           │ process performs 10 iterations of these three actions.             │
           │                                                                    │
           │ The transition logic doesn't differentiate between actions of      │
           │ loading `x`, incrementing `x`, and storing `x` after the           │
           │ increment. This can lead to the increment operation not correctly  │
           │ representing the concurrent nature of the processes.               │
           │                                                                    │
           │ block: Initial values and inconsistencies                          │
           │ fix: The initialization of `x` in the `Parallel` class is not      │
           │ taking into account since it's duplicated in both `P2` and         │
           │ `Process` classes. Moreover, the `specification` section checks    │
           │ the invariants assuming processes maintain their own `x` (p1.x,    │
           │ p2.x, p3.x), which is inconsistent with the concept of shared      │
           │ variable `x` across all processes. The initial value of `x` should │
           │ be shared and consistently referenced across the modules.          │
           │                                                                    │
           │ block: Suggestions for fixing                                      │
           │ fix: 1. **Shared Variable Declaration:** Ensure that a single      │
           │ shared variable `x` is used across all modules. Remove individual  │
           │ `x` declarations from `P1`, `P2`, `P3`, and `Process` classes,     │
           │ keeping it centralized in the `Parallel` class.                    │
           │                                                                    │
           │ 2. **Parallel Execution Logic:** Modify the `next` function to     │
           │ incorporate the loading, incrementing, and storing activities for  │
           │ `x`. Each process should execute a loop mimicking `for x =         │
           │ 1,...,10, do LOAD(x); INC(x); STORE(x); od`. Wrap each segment     │
           │ with proper control structure in order to simulate concurrency.    │
           │                                                                    │
           │ 3. **Correct Invariant Specifications:** Adjust the invariants to  │
           │ reflect actions on the shared variable `x`. Assertions like        │
           │ `(self.p1.x == 2) or (self.p2.x == 2) or (self.p3.x == 2)` should  │
           │ be based on a global `x` variable being checked by the shared      │
           │ terminal invariant `x_final_state_inv = (self.x == 2)`.            │
           │                                                                    │
           │ 4. **Semaphore/Locks for Synchronization:** Introduce              │
           │ synchronization constructs like semaphores or locks in the model   │
           │ to handle the concurrent modification of the shared variable `x`   │
           │ by the processes, ensuring data consistency and race-free          │
           │ execution.                                                         │
